Our verify rows do not share a KV scan: QSA selects each row's own top-k cells (`sel_` per row, `verify.cpp`), only a dense / identity selection (the drafter, short contexts) is the same for every row, and the one-row WMMA kernel already fills M with the 12 heads of a kv head, so hipfire's shared-K-scan layout has nothing to share here and a faithful port would need all R rows' Q fragments in VGPRs (64 VGPRs a row: R = 4 is 256, the whole file). What IS coded: `STRATA_ATTN_WMMA_ROWS=2 / 4 / 8` (with `STRATA_ATTN_WMMA=1`; `decode_attn_wmma_rows_kernel`, `qsa_decode_attn_wmma.cu`): up to R rows of one `qsa_decode_attn_batch` call per block; per row the same instruction sequence as the one-row kernel, so bit-identical outputs are expected; the staged V rows and scales are read once per group when the rows' selections are identical for the chunk (same width, same cells), else restaged. Honest scope: it saves launches and V staging, not K traffic; it can lose (fewer blocks per launch, and the int8 rows kernel spills 49 VGPRs against 0, T-878). The parity harness `hip_decode_attn_wmma_parity` now compares 2 / 4 / 8 rows with the one-row kernel byte for byte (and two shared-selection cases); CODED, UNTESTED on a GPU, `conversation_disk_env.def` Speed. Specs 192 (screen + confirm, judged against `wmma`, not `base`) and 193 (token forcing). Promotion waits for box run; nothing to promote without a result. STOP POINT: hipfire's WMMA split-K kernel itself (Q fragments in VGPRs, two key-half streams) is not adapted: our rows have different cells, so the stop is a data fact, not a lack of time; revisit only if a verify-window trace shows identical selections across rows. Coded as the build macro `STRATA_ATTN_LAZY_RESCALE=T` (T = 1..8 log2 units, default 0): the running maximum moves only when a chunk exceeds it by more than T, alpha is exactly 1 otherwise, the V scale headroom shrinks by 2^T (`STRATA_PA_VTOP`) so p * vs * vup stays inside FP16 (hipfire's f16 range works without that scale; ours would overflow above T = 1 without it), and a wave whose 8 alphas are all 1 skips the multiply. The skip has a price: T-879 the TR=0 int8 kernels spill 66 / 90 / 98 VGPRs (default 0 / 10 / 13), so `STRATA_ATTN_LAZY_NOSKIP` (the lazy maximum only, resources identical to the default) is the variant to try if the skip loses. | L14 MoE prefill grouped WMMA with the activation tile shared by 4 waves through double-buffered LDS, weights straight from global (hipfire hipfire:kernels/gemm_mq4g256v2_moe_grouped_k2_pm_gfx1100.hip, variant R1Moe-04-xlds-w4-t2: 64 rows per block, tile run 2, prefetch 1, one barrier per K64 chunk, global X traffic / 4, "predicted candidate / shipped time ratio 0.5", card-judged by a generator and sealed bit-identical) `[read]` | predicted 0.5 of the shipped kernel's time (their prediction; no measured number in the header) | exact (bit-identical to their shipped kernel) | OURS: MoE prefill goes through the ggml MMQ id kernels (J=32 spill pragma, T-579) and our F16 prompt kernels (`PF_F16=1` adopted: +15-17% long prompt, T-820). Coded: build macro `STRATA_PF_MOE_XLDS` in `native_f16_kernel` (a second stage buffer, one barrier per stage, same arithmetic; the 128-row tile keeps one buffer because 2 x 34 KB exceeds the 64 KB limit; T-880: LDS up 13.5-40.6 -> 26.9-51.7 KB, waves/SIMD 6-16 -> 4-8). The K-quant kernels are not changed. Spec 194 arm `xlds`. CODED, UNTESTED; promotion waits for box run (occupancy may eat the barrier saving). Already in our tree, so no row: the MTP draft-vocabulary subset (their #998, 65,536 tokens, +7.4-9.4% decode on top of the other two: ours is `data/draft_vocab*.bin`, English 40.5K best, full and zh60k flat or worse, T-109, T-121, T-123, T-146); int8-activation GEMV for 2-5 verify rows (#987: our verify GEMVs are the ggml MMVQ q8_1 path and `STRATA_MMVF_ROWS1`); MTP checkpoint ring and window repair after EOS or a tool call (their 126 s -> 29 s at 135K tokens, a 19,729-token rewind 50.7 s -> 10.6 s: ours is `conversation_cache.hpp` strict-prefix reuse, `CKPT_*`, T-575 family); `HIPFIRE_HIP_WAIT=spin` (rejected there; ours sets `cudaDeviceScheduleSpin`, generate.cpp:4724); the CPU-splice facts (int8 activations a null because the splice streams weights at ~26 GB/s of the box's 46-55 GB/s, a batched multi-expert GEMV region +47%, prefix-broadcast 3/6-bit decode +4.7%, adaptive rayon grain flat: our pool is C++ with `EXPDEC` and the hugetlb copy; the bandwidth probe `tools/pancake/probes/expert_bw.py` was never run, MOD_PLAN.md 4.2.3); MTP depth priced only from measured per-arch costs (lessons section 5: ours `DraftPolicy::cost_ms` plus the union fit `STRATA_DRAFT_UFAC` for unmeasured widths, T-762); Qwen3.5/3.6 dense items, EP2 / TP expert and tensor parallelism, the NPU, vision, image generation and the gfx942 route mirror (no second GPU and no CDNA here: parked, hardware this box does not have).
